### Community 195 - "ADR-0011 — Capability ports for cross-module collaboration"
Cohesion: 0.14
Nodes (21): ADR-0002 — Bun-only runtime & tooling, Bun-only policy (rationale: two runtimes add behavioural branching, toolchain size and bug surface; node/npm/npx/pnpm/yarn and Node-forcing adapters are forbidden, node:* imports allowed, node-shebang bins invoked with bun --bun; trade-off: Astro has no first-party Bun adapter so SSR uses a Bun.serve seam or @astrojs/node as a sanctioned exception), ADR-0007 — OpenAPI & AsyncAPI as mandatory contracts, Design-first contract as source of truth — openapi/ for REST, asyncapi/ for domain events, validated by api:spec:check so every event in a module's publishes array is a registered AsyncAPI channel (rationale: generating contracts from code only was rejected because the contract is used before the code exists; no event contract was rejected because cross-module events are fragile and hard to test), ADR-0008 — Independent versioning: package, API/event contract, module descriptor, Module descriptor status vocabulary — experimental / active / deprecated, purely descriptive metadata not consumed by any runtime, so changing it carries zero behavioural risk, Three independent version schemes — package.json release SemVer (Changesets), OpenAPI/AsyncAPI info.version (contract shape), module descriptor version/status (module maturity) (rationale: mechanically aligning all three would falsely signal the contract/module changes whenever anything in the repo changes; 1.0.0 declares stability for production consumption, not 'first release'), ADR-0011 — Capability ports for cross-module collaboration (+13 more)

Cohesion: 0.40
